Result: CNN-only模型在多脚本训练下改进有限或不稳定，而CRNN模型在数据最受限的场景中取得了更好的性能；跨语言改进与序列级建模相关，仅共享CNN编码器学习的视觉表示（字符形状相似性）不足以实现有效迁移。

Conclusion: 上下文建模（序列级依赖）在低资源场景下实现有效迁移中起关键作用，这一发现可能扩展到其他低资源语言设置。

Abstract: Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) for Arabic-script languages benefits from cross-language joint training under low-resource conditions, particularly when using CRNN-based models that combine convolutional encoders with sequence modeling. However, it remains unclear whether these improvements are better explained by shared visual representations or sequence-level dependencies. In this work, we conduct a controlled architectural study of line-level Arabic-script HTR, comparing CNN-only models with CTC decoding and CRNN models under identical single-script and multi-script training regimes. Experiments are performed on Arabic (KHATT), Urdu (NUST-UHWR), and Persian (PHTD) datasets under low-resource settings (K in {100, 500, 1000}). Our results show a clear divergence in transfer behavior: while CNN-only models exhibit limited or unstable improvements, CRNN models achieve better performance under multi-script training, particularly in the most data-constrained regimes. Focusing on transfer improvements (delta CER) rather than absolute performance, we find that cross-language improvements are associated with sequence-level modeling, while sharing visual representations learned by the CNN encoder, corresponding to similarities in character shapes across scripts, alone appears to be insufficient. This finding suggests that contextual modeling plays an important role in enabling effective transfer in low-resource scenarios, and that similar behavior may extend to other low-resource language settings.

</details>


### [39] [Architecture-agnostic Lipschitz-constant Bayesian header and its application to resolve semantically proximal classification errors with vision transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05908)
*Frederik Schäfer,Luis Mandl,Lars Kälber,Tim Ricken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种架构无关的Lipschitz约束贝叶斯头部（LipB-ViT），通过谱归一化变分权重的均值和log方差来提升模型在标签噪声（尤其是语义邻近错误）下的鲁棒性，在15%语义错误标签下召回率超0.93，较SOTA提升7%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签噪声（特别是结构化的语义邻近分类错误）严重制约了监督深度学习模型的泛化能力，而现有鲁棒训练方法在此类噪声下表现不佳。

Method: 提出Lipschitz常数约束的贝叶斯头部，集成到特征提取器中形成LipB-ViT；在变分权重的均值和log方差上施加谱归一化；提出联合捕捉不确定性与置信度的新指标；以及自适应算术均值融合方案。

Result: 在15%语义误标下，召回率超0.93，较最先进的k近邻方法提升7%以上；在结构化与非结构化噪声下均展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LipB-ViT作为即插即用方法，跨领域超参数一致，适用于标注可靠性多变的高风险应用场景。

Abstract: Label noise remains a critical bottleneck for the generalization of supervised deep learning models, particularly when errors are structured rather than random. Standard robust training methods often fail in the presence of such semantically proximal classification errors. This work presents an architecture-agnostic Lipschitz-constant Bayesian header that can be integrated into feature extractors such as vision transformers, yielding the bi-Lipschitz-constrained Bayesian Vision Transformer (LipB-ViT). In contrast to conventional Bayesian layers, our approach enforces spectral normalization on both the mean and log-variance of the variational weights, which promotes calibrated predictive uncertainty and mitigates noise amplification. We further propose a novel metric to jointly capture uncertainty and confidence across misclassification rates, as well as an adaptive arithmetic-mean fusion scheme that combines feature-space proximity with predictive uncertainty to detect corrupted labels outperforming the state of the art k-nearest neighbor based identification methods by more than 7% reaching a recall of more than 0.93 at 15% semantically misclassified labels. Although computational costs increase due to Monte Carlo sampling, the method offers plug-and-play compatibility with pre-trained backbones and consistent hyperparameters across domains, suggesting strong utility for high-stakes applications with variable annotation reliability. The stabilized confidence estimates serve as the foundation for an analysis pipeline that jointly assesses dataset quality and label noise, yielding a second novel metric for their combined quantification. Lastly, we systematically evaluate LipB-ViT under both structured (adversarial) and unstructured noise at inference time, demonstrating its robustness in realistic high-noise and attack scenarios. We compare its performance against baseline methods.

</details>


### [40] [Plug-and-play Class-aware Knowledge Injection for Prompt Learning with Visual-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05910)
*Junhui Yin,Nan Pu,Xinyu Zhang,Lingfeng Yang,Lin Wu,Xiaojie Wang,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAKI框架，通过生成类特定提示并存储到知识库，在测试时检索匹配注入类级知识，提升VLM零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习方法要么使用类共享提示（过于粗糙），要么生成实例特定提示（忽略类级信息），都未充分利用类特定知识，导致分类效果次优。

Method: 提出CAKI框架，包含两个模块：1) 类特定提示生成——从同类的少样本样本中编码类特定知识，存入类级知识库；2) 查询-键提示匹配——为每个测试实例从知识库检索相关类级知识并注入以优化预测。

Result: 大量实验表明CAKI能有效提升现有方法在基类和新类上的分类性能。

Conclusion: CAKI作为即插即用框架，通过注入类特定知识显著增强VLM零样本分类能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Prompt learning has become an effective and widely used technique in enhancing vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP for various downstream tasks, particularly in zero-shot classification within specific domains. Existing methods typically focus on either learning class-shared prompts for a given domain or generating instance-specific prompts through conditional prompt learning. While these methods have achieved promising performance, they often overlook class-specific knowledge in prompt design, leading to suboptimal outcomes. The underlying reasons are: 1) class-specific prompts offer more fine-grained supervision compared to coarse class-shared prompts, which helps prevent misclassification of data from different classes into a single class; 2) compared to class-specific prompts, instance-specific prompts neglect the richer class-level information across multiple instances, potentially causing data from the same class to be divided into multiple classes. To effectively supplement the class-specific knowledge into existing methods, we propose a plug-and-play Class-Aware Knowledge Injection (CAKI) framework. CAKI comprises two key components, i.e., class-specific prompt generation and query-key prompt matching. The former encodes class-specific knowledge into prompts from few-shot samples that belong to the same class and stores the learned prompts in a class-level knowledge bank. The latter provides a plug-and-play mechanism for each test instance to retrieve relevant class-level knowledge from the knowledge bank and inject such knowledge to refine model predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our CAKI effectively improves the performance of existing methods on base and novel classes. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/yjh576/CAKI}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [41] [Think, then Score: Decoupled Reasoning and Scoring for Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05922)
*Yuan Wang,Ouxiang Li,Yulong Xu,Borui Liao,Jiajun Liang,Jinghan Li,Meng Wang,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wang,Kuien Liu,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeScore，一种解耦的“先思考后评分”视频奖励模型，结合生成式CoT推理与判别式评分模块，通过两阶段训练提升泛化能力与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有判别式奖励模型缺乏显式推理，易受捷径学习影响；生成式奖励模型虽具有推理可解释性，但推理与评分耦合导致优化瓶颈。需一种兼顾两者优势的奖励模型。

Method: DeScore采用解耦的“think-then-score”架构：MLLM先生成显式CoT推理，再由可学习查询标记与回归头组成的判别式评分模块预测奖励。两阶段训练：(1)含随机掩码机制的判别式冷启动，(2)双目标强化学习分别优化CoT推理质量与最终奖励校准。

Result: DeScore在训练效率与泛化能力上优于现有判别式与生成式奖励模型，高质量推理直接转化为更优的模型性能。

Conclusion: 解耦推理与评分是提升视频奖励模型性能的有效范式，双目标强化学习可确保推理质量与评分精度协同提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models are increasingly driven by post-training and test-time scaling, both of which critically depend on the quality of video reward models (RMs). An ideal reward model should predict accurate rewards that align with human preferences across diverse scenarios. However, existing paradigms face a fundamental dilemma: \textit{Discriminative RMs} regress rewards directly on features extracted by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) without explicit reasoning, making them prone to shortcut learning and heavily reliant on massive data scaling for generalization. In contrast, \textit{Generative RMs} with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning exhibit superior interpretability and generalization potential, as they leverage fine-grained semantic supervision to internalize the rationales behind human preferences. However, they suffer from inherent optimization bottlenecks due to the coupling of reasoning and scoring within a single autoregressive inference chain. To harness the generalization benefits of CoT reasoning while mitigating the training instability of coupled reasoning and scoring, we introduce DeScore, a training-efficient and generalizable video reward model. DeScore employs a decoupled ``think-then-score'' paradigm: an MLLM first generates an explicit CoT, followed by a dedicated discriminative scoring module consisting of a learnable query token and a regression head that predicts the final reward. DeScore is optimized via a two-stage framework: (1) a discriminative cold start incorporating a random mask mechanism to ensure robust scoring capabilities, and (2) a dual-objective reinforcement learning stage that independently refines CoT reasoning quality and calibrates the final reward, ensuring that higher-quality reasoning directly translates to superior model performance.

</details>


### [42] [Backdoor Mitigation in Object Detection via Adversarial Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05928)
*Kealan Dunnett,Reza Arablouei,Dimity Miller,Volkan Dedeoglu,Raja Jurdak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向目标检测的检测感知对抗微调框架，用于在仅知受损检测器和少量干净数据的情况下防御后门攻击，通过软分支最小化和双目标微调损失有效降低攻击成功率并保持检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测的后门防御研究远落后于图像分类，现有的分类导向对抗微调方法不适用于目标检测的复杂攻击空间（如目标消失或误分类），且标准检测损失会稀释修复信号。

Method: 提出检测感知对抗微调框架：1）软分支最小化（Soft-Branch Minimisation）通过软门控组合针对误分类和消失攻击的目标，结合检测感知的分类损失最大化；2）双目标微调损失（Dual-Objective Fine-tuning Loss）作用于目标匹配的预测，集中防御更新于与后门行为最相关的预测上。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer检测器上的实验表明，该方法相比分类导向基线，更有效地降低了攻击成功率，同时保留了真实检测结果，并保持了有竞争力的干净检测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的检测感知对抗微调框架有效解决了目标检测后门防御的适配难题，在不需知道攻击目标的情况下实现了有效且高效的后门缓解。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks can implant malicious behaviours into deep models while preserving performance on clean data, posing a serious threat to safety-critical vision systems. Although backdoor mitigation has been studied extensively for image classification, defenses for object detection remain comparatively underdeveloped. Adversarial fine-tuning is a common backdoor mitigation approach in classification, but adapting it to detection is nontrivial as classification-oriented adversarial generation does not match the detection attack space, where attacks may cause object misclassification or disappearance, and standard detection losses can dilute the repair signal across many predictions. We address these challenges through a detection-aware adversarial fine-tuning framework for mitigating object-detection backdoors when the defender has access only to a compromised detector and a small clean dataset, without knowing the attack objective. For adversarial generation that does not require knowledge of the attack objective, we introduce soft-branch minimisation, which uses a soft gate to combine objectives aligned with misclassification and disappearance attacks, together with a detection-aware classification-loss maximisation. For targeted repair, we introduce a dual-objective fine-tuning loss applied to target-matched predictions, concentrating the defensive update on predictions most relevant to the backdoor behaviour. Experiments across CNN- and Transformer-based detectors show that our approach more effectively reduces attack success while preserving true detections, compared with classification-oriented baselines, and maintains competitive clean detection performance.

</details>


### [43] [Whole-body CT attenuation and volume charts from routine clinical scans via evidence-grounded LLM report filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05933)
*Christian Wachinger,Bernhard Renger,Christopher Späth,Jan Kirschke,Marcus Makowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）集成的方法，从放射学报告中过滤病理发现，从而在超过35万次CT检查中构建减少病理干扰的队列。通过五种LLM交叉验证异常标志，结合分布感知的广义可加模型，建立了覆盖106个解剖结构（体积和衰减）的全生命周期参考图谱，支持协变量调整的百分位数评分，用于标准化定量表型分析和机会性筛查研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床CT数据集通常富含病理信息，导致难以构建用于解释定量CT生物标志物（如器官体积和组织衰减）的大规模健康参考分布。现有方法缺乏有效过滤病理发现的手段，限制了标准化定量表型分析和多中心影像研究的发展。

Method: 1) 使用五种大语言模型（LLM）对放射学报告进行结构级异常候选标志，基于逐字证据；2) 通过交叉验证机制解决不同LLM之间的分歧；3) 采用分布感知的广义可加模型（GAMLSS）建立参考图表，涵盖年龄、性别、造影增强和采集参数等因素。

Result: 建立了覆盖106个解剖结构（体积和衰减）的全生命周期参考图表，纵向分析揭示了结构与造影剂相关的变化，与横截面趋势存在差异。这些资源支持协变量调整的百分位数评分，可用于标准化定量表型分析、多中心影像研究和可扩展的机会性筛查研究。

Conclusion: 本文开发的LLM集成过滤方法能够有效从大规模CT数据中构建减少病理干扰的队列，并结合GAMLSS模型建立了全面的全身参考图谱。该方法为临床CT中的标准化定量评估和机会性筛查提供了可靠工具，具有重要的研究应用价值。

Abstract: Interpreting quantitative CT biomarkers, such as organ volume and tissue attenuation, requires large-scale healthy reference distributions. However, creating these is challenging because clinical datasets are often heavily enriched with pathology. Here, we develop an evidence-grounded, cross-verified large language model (LLM) ensemble to filter pathological findings from radiology reports, enabling the construction of pathology-reduced cohorts from over 350,000 CT examinations. Five LLMs, first, flag structure-level abnormality candidates grounded in verbatim report evidence and, second, resolve disagreements via cross-verification. Using distribution-aware generalized additive models for location, scale, and shape, we establish comprehensive whole-body reference charts for 106 anatomical structures (volumes and attenuation) across adulthood, accounting for age, sex, contrast enhancement, and acquisition parameters. Longitudinal analyses reveal structure- and contrast-dependent changes distinct from cross-sectional trends. These resources facilitate covariate-adjusted centile scoring from routine CT, supporting standardized quantitative phenotyping, multi-site imaging studies, and scalable opportunistic screening research.

</details>


### [44] [RAWild: Sensor-Agnostic RAW Object Detection via Physics-Guided Curve and Grid Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05941)
*Shuhong Liu,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Yinqiang Zheng,Tatsuya Harada,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAWild框架，通过物理引导的全局-局部色调映射实现跨传感器RAW图像目标检测，在多种RAW基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAW数据相比sRGB具有位深更深、保留物理信息、无ISP失真等优势，但不同设备的曝光条件、光谱灵敏度和位深差异导致传感器间的域间隙远大于sRGB，使得传感器无关的泛化成为根本性挑战。

Method: 提出RAWild框架，采用物理引导的全局-局部色调映射，将传感器引起的变化分解为全局色调校正和空间自适应局部颜色调整，两者均由RAW分布先验驱动；同时构建基于物理的RAW模拟管道，合成覆盖多种光谱灵敏度、光照和传感器非理想性的真实传感器输出。

Result: 在涵盖10到24位深的多个RAW基准上，在单数据集、混合数据集和鲁棒性设置下均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RAWild框架通过物理引导的色调映射和RAW模拟管道有效解决了跨传感器泛化问题，实现了传感器无关的RAW目标检测。

Abstract: Camera sensor RAW data offers intrinsic advantages for object detection, including deeper bit depth, preserved physical information, and freedom from image signal processor (ISP) distortions. However, varying exposure conditions, spectral sensitivities, and bit depths across devices introduce substantially larger domain gaps than sRGB, making sensor-agnostic generalization a fundamental challenge. In this study, we present \textbf{RAWild}, a physics-guided global-local tone mapping framework for sensor-agnostic RAW object detection. By factoring sensor-induced variations into a global tonal correction and a spatially adaptive local color adjustment, both driven by RAW distribution priors, our framework enables a single network to train jointly across heterogeneous sensors. To further support cross-sensor generalization, we construct a physics-based RAW simulation pipeline that synthesizes realistic sensor outputs spanning diverse spectral sensitivities, illuminants, and sensor non-idealities. Extensive experiments across multiple RAW benchmarks covering bit depths from 10 to 24 demonstrate state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance under single-dataset, mixed-dataset, and challenging robustness settings.

</details>


### [45] [MobileEgo Anywhere: Open Infrastructure for long horizon egocentric data on commodity hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05945)
*Senthil Palanisamy,Abhishek Anand,Satpal Singh Rathor,Pratyush Patnaik,Shubhanshu Khatana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileEgo Anywhere框架，利用智能手机传感器实现大规模、长时间（小时级）的第一人称数据采集，释放了200小时数据集、开源移动应用及处理流水线，以支持视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的训练，降低了机器人数据采集的硬件门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称数据集大多只有几分钟的短片段，无法捕捉复杂机器人任务所需的长程时序依赖关系，且传统数据采集方法硬件门槛高，难以大规模获取长程数据。

Method: 利用现代智能手机内置的传感器套件（如IMU、摄像头）实现高保真的长程相机位姿跟踪；开发移动端数据采集应用；构建标准化数据处理流水线，将原始手机采集数据转换为VLA模型可直接使用的训练格式。

Result: 发布了包含200小时多样化的长程第一人称数据集的成果，并开源了移动应用和完整的处理流水线。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将数据采集过程民主化，能够在全球多样化的环境中大规模获取长程数据，从而加速通用机器人策略的开发。

Abstract: The recent advancement of Vision Language Action (VLA) models has driven a critical demand for large scale egocentric datasets. However, existing datasets are often limited by short episode durations, typically spanning only a few minutes, which fails to capture the long horizon temporal dependencies necessary for complex robotic task execution. To bridge this gap, we present MobileEgo Anywhere, a framework designed to facilitate the collection of robust, hour plus egocentric trajectories using commodity mobile hardware. We leverage the ubiquitous sensor suites of modern smartphones to provide high fidelity, long term camera pose tracking, effectively removing the high hardware barriers associated with traditional robotics data collection. Our contributions are three fold: (1) we release a novel dataset comprising 200 hours of diverse, long form egocentric data with persistent state tracking; (2) we open source a mobile application that enables any user to record egocentric data, and (3) we provide a comprehensive processing pipeline to convert raw mobile captures into standardized, training ready formats for Vision Language Action model and foundation model research. By democratizing the data collection process, this work enables the massive scale acquisition of long horizon data across varied global environments, accelerating the development of generalizable robotic policies.

</details>


### [46] [Prompt-Free and Efficient SAM2 Adaptation for Biomedical Semantic Segmentation via Dual Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05979)
*Hinako Mitsuoka,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向医学图像分割的无提示、参数高效微调框架，通过卷积位置编码生成器和双适配器策略，在多个数据集上显著优于SAM2基线，准确率最高提升19.66%，计算成本降低约87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM2在自然图像上表现优异，但在医学图像分割中面临领域漂移和提示依赖问题，亟需一种无需提示、参数高效的微调方法。

Method: 1) 引入卷积位置编码生成器适配任意长宽比输入；2) 提出双适配器策略：高性能适配器（可变形卷积）用于精确边界建模，轻量适配器（结构重参数化）降低推理延迟。

Result: 在ISBI 2012、Kvasir-SEG、Synapse和ACDC数据集上，相比原始SAM2分割准确率提升高达19.66%，计算成本较医学SAM适配方法降低约87%。

Conclusion: 该方法在医学图像分割中实现了准确率与效率的优越权衡，验证了无提示、参数高效微调框架的有效性。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) demonstrated impressive zero-shot capabilities on natural images but faces challenges in biomedical segmentation due to significant domain shifts and prompt dependency. To address these limitations, we propose a prompt-free, parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework designed for multi-class segmentation on variable-sized inputs. We introduce a convolutional Positional Encoding Generator to adapt effectively to arbitrary aspect ratios and present a dual-adapter strategy: High-Performance Adapter utilizing deformable convolutions for precise boundary modeling and Lightweight Adapter employing structural re-parameterization to minimize inference latency. Experiments on ISBI 2012, Kvasir-SEG, Synapse, and ACDC datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms strong adaptation baselines. Specifically, our method improved segmentation accuracy by up to 19.66\% over the vanilla SAM2, while reducing computational costs by approximately 87\% compared to heavyweight medical SAM adaptations, establishing a superior trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [47] [iPhoneBlur: A Difficulty-Stratified Benchmark for Consumer Device Motion Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05990)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Kazi Saeed Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了iPhoneBlur基准，包含7,400张按模糊难度分层的图像对，用于评估移动设备上的运动模糊恢复模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法使用聚合指标掩盖了模型在不同模糊难度下的性能差异，无法反映真实部署条件下的模型行为。

Method: 从iPhone 17 Pro高帧率视频中合成7,400对图像，通过PSNR引导的自适应时间窗口将样本分为Easy、Medium、Hard三个难度层级，并附有丰富元数据。

Result: 六种架构在Easy到Hard子集上表现出一致的7-9 dB性能下降，这一巨大差距被聚合指标完全隐藏；还暴露了专业相机与消费相机之间的领域差距。

Conclusion: 通过难度分层与部署关键元数据的结合，iPhoneBlur能够系统评估资源受限边缘系统中模型的可靠性与故障模式。

Abstract: Motion blur restoration on consumer mobile devices is typically evaluated using aggregate metrics that obscure performance variation across blur difficulty, masking model behavior under real deployment conditions. This work introduces iPhoneBlur, a difficulty-stratified benchmark of 7,400 image pairs synthesized from high-framerate iPhone 17 Pro videos captured in diverse real-world scenarios. Samples are partitioned into Easy, Medium, and Hard categories through PSNR-guided adaptive temporal windowing, with stratification validated by monotonic 2.2x increase in optical flow magnitude across tiers. Each sample includes comprehensive metadata enabling investigation of ISP-aware and difficulty-adaptive restoration strategies. Spectral analysis confirms synthesized blur exhibits high-frequency suppression patterns consistent with authentic motion degradation. Evaluation of six architectures reveals consistent 7-9 dB performance degradation from Easy to Hard subsets, a substantial gap entirely hidden by aggregate reporting. The benchmark further exposes a domain gap between professional and consumer cameras which targeted fine-tuning substantially recovers. By coupling difficulty stratification with deployment-critical metadata, iPhoneBlur enables systematic assessment of model reliability and failure modes for resource-constrained edge systems.

</details>


### [48] [4DThinker: Thinking with 4D Imagery for Dynamic Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05997)
*Zhangquan Chen,Manyuan Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Xiang An,Bo Li,Xin Xie,ZiDong Wang,Mingze Sun,Shuang Chen,Hongyu Li,Xiaobin Hu,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4DThinker框架，让视觉语言模型(VLM)通过在连续隐空间中模拟场景动态（即“4D思维”）进行动态空间推理，无需外部几何模块。通过无标注数据生成、动态意象微调(DIFT)和4D强化学习(4DRL)，在多个基准上超越强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将时空推理完全文本化（冗长且不精确），要么依赖外部几何模块（增加推理复杂度且无法提升模型内在能力），因此亟需一种让VLM自身具备动态空间推理能力的方法。

Method: 1) 从原始视频中合成4D推理数据的无标注数据生成管线；2) 动态意象微调(DIFT)，联合监督文本token和4D隐变量；3) 4D强化学习(4DRL)，通过基于结果奖励的策略梯度优化，仅更新文本token以保证稳定性。

Result: 在多个动态空间推理基准上，4DThinker一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 4DThinker为VLM的4D推理提供了新视角，证明了让模型在隐空间中动态“想象”场景演变是可行的。

Abstract: Dynamic spatial reasoning from monocular video is essential for bridging visual intelligence and the physical world, yet remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs). Prior approaches either verbalize spatial-temporal reasoning entirely as text, which is inherently verbose and imprecise for complex dynamics, or rely on external geometric modules that increase inference complexity without fostering intrinsic model capability. In this paper, we present 4DThinker, the first framework that enables VLMs to "think with 4D" through dynamic latent mental imagery, i.e., internally simulating how scenes evolve within the continuous hidden space. Specifically, we first introduce a scalable, annotation-free data generation pipeline that synthesizes 4D reasoning data from raw videos. We then propose Dynamic-Imagery Fine-Tuning (DIFT), which jointly supervises textual tokens and 4D latents to ground the model in dynamic visual semantics. Building on this, 4D Reinforcement Learning (4DRL) further tackles complex reasoning tasks via outcome-based rewards, restricting policy gradients to text tokens to ensure stable optimization. Extensive experiments across multiple dynamic spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that 4DThinker consistently outperforms strong baselines and offers a new perspective toward 4D reasoning in VLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangquanchen/4DThinker.

</details>


### [49] [T2I-VeRW: Part-level Fine-grained Perception for Text-to-Image Vehicle Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06012)
*Xiao Wang,Ziwen Wang,Weizhe Kong,Wentao Wu,Yuehang Li,Aihua Zheng,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PFCVR模型，通过部件级局部对齐和双向掩码恢复模块实现文本到图像的车辆再识别，同时构建了包含细粒度部件级标注的大规模数据集T2I-VeRW，在多个基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆再识别主要基于图像查询，但在实际场景中往往只有目击者的文本描述可用，因此需要将车辆再识别从纯图像查询扩展到基于文本的查询，实现跨模态车辆检索。

Method: 1) 提出部件级局部对齐方法，引入可学习的部件查询令牌，聚合部件特定和全句上下文信息；2) 设计双向掩码恢复模块，让每个模态在另一模态指导下恢复其掩码内容，隐式地将局部对应桥接为全局特征对齐；3) 构建新的大规模数据集T2I-VeRW，包含14,668张图像和1,796个车辆身份，带有细粒度部件级标注。

Result: 在T2I-VeRI数据集上达到29.2% Rank-1准确率，比最优方法提升+3.7%；在新提出的T2I-VeRW基准上达到55.2% Rank-1准确率，优于多种最新方法。

Conclusion: PFCVR通过部件级细粒度跨模态对齐有效提升了文本到图像车辆再识别的性能，新构建的数据集也为该领域提供了更有挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Vehicle Re-identification (Re-ID) aims to retrieve the most similar image to a given query from images captured by non-overlapping cameras. Extending vehicle Re-ID from image-only queries to text-based queries enables retrieval in real-world scenarios where only a witness description of the target vehicle is available. In this paper, we propose PFCVR, a Part-level Fine-grained Cross-modal Vehicle Retrieval model for text-to-image vehicle re-identification. PFCVR constructs locally paired images and texts at the part level and introduces learnable part-query tokens that aggregate both part-specific and full-sentence context before aligning with visual part features. On top of this explicit local alignment, a bi-directional mask recovery module lets each modality reconstruct its masked content under the guidance of the other, implicitly bridging local correspondences into global feature alignment. Furthermore, we construct a new large-scale dataset called T2I-VeRW, which contains 14,668 images covering 1,796 vehicle identities with fine-grained part-level annotations. Experimental results on the T2I-VeRI dataset show that PFCVR achieves 29.2\% Rank-1 accuracy, improving over the best competing method by +3.7\% percentage points. On the newly proposed T2I-VeRW benchmark, PFCVR achieves 55.2\% Rank-1 accuracy, outperforming a comprehensive set of recent state-of-the-art methods. Source code will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/Neuromorphic_ReID

</details>


### [50] [PlotPick: AI-powered batch extraction of numerical data from scientific figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06021)
*Tommy Carstensen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlotPick是一个利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从科学图表中批量提取结构化表格数据的开源工具，在ChartX和PlotQA基准测试中，六种VLM均优于专门的图表转表格模型DePlot，尤其在DePlot未见过的图表类型（如箱线图）上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述和元分析经常需要从作者仅以图片形式报告的图表中提取数值数据，但手动数字化速度慢且难以规模化，因此需要自动化的批量提取工具。

Method: 开发了PlotPick开源工具，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）实现从科学图表中批量提取结构化表格数据；在ChartX和PlotQA两个基准数据集上评估了来自三家提供商的六种VLM，并与专门的图表转表格模型DePlot进行对比。

Result: 在ChartX（n=300，限于柱状图、折线图、箱线图和直方图）上，VLM的召回率达到88-96%，DePlot为71%；在PlotQA（n=529）上，VLM的RMSF1达到86-99%，DePlot为94%。在DePlot未见过的箱线图上，DePlot仅达到24%的RMSF1，而VLM达到83-97%。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在图表数据提取任务上显著优于专门的图表转表格模型，尤其在训练数据中未出现的图表类型上表现突出，表明VLM具有更强的泛化能力。PlotPick作为开源工具可供使用。

Abstract: Systematic reviews and meta-analyses frequently require numerical data that authors report only as figures, yet manual digitisation is slow and does not scale. We present PlotPick, an open-source tool that uses vision-language models (VLMs) to batch-extract structured tabular data from scientific figures. We evaluate six VLMs from three providers on two established chart-to-table benchmarks (ChartX and PlotQA) and compare against the dedicated chart-to-table model DePlot. All six VLMs outperform DePlot on both benchmarks. On ChartX (restricted to bar charts, line charts, box plots, and histograms; n=300), VLMs achieve 88-96% recall versus 71% for DePlot. On PlotQA (n=529), VLMs achieve 86-99% RMSF1 versus 94% for DePlot. The gap is largest on chart types absent from the dedicated models' training data: on box plots, DePlot achieves 24% RMSF1 while VLMs achieve 83-97%. PlotPick is available at https://plotpick.streamlit.app.

</details>


### [51] [Domain Generalization through Spatial Relation Induction over Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06043)
*Dat Nguyen,Duc-Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PARSE框架，将视觉识别分解为原始基元及其关系组合，通过可学习的空间谓词和端到端架构提升领域泛化性能，在CUB-DG上提升超4.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域泛化方法多通过改进训练过程（如模型选择、数据增强、特征对齐）来寻找稳定表征，但将结构组成的表征学习隐式化，限制了在组合性领域泛化基准上的性能。

Method: 提出PARSE框架：1) CNN骨干提取通用视觉特征；2) 概念瓶颈层将特征映射为带有可微分空间坐标的原始基元热力图；3) 结构评分层评估基元间的候选空间关系，利用软二元、三元、四元谓词表示空间对齐，端到端学习。

Result: 在CUB-DG上准确率提升超4.5个百分点，在DomainBed基准上与现有方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将视觉识别显式分解为原始基元及其关系组合的结构化方法，能有效提升领域泛化性能，特别是在组合性领域泛化任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Domain generalization requires identifying stable representations that support reliable classification across domains. Most existing methods seek such stability through improving the training process, for example, through model selection strategies, data augmentation, or feature-alignment objectives. Although these strategies can be effective, they leave the representation learning of structural composition implicit, which may limit performance on compositional domain generalization benchmarks.
  In this work, we propose Primitive-Aware Relational Structure for domain gEneralization (PARSE), an image classification framework that factors visual recognition into visual primitives and their relational composition. We represent these compositions using soft binary, ternary, and quaternary predicates over primitive locations, yielding differentiable measures of spatial alignment that can be learned end-to-end. To learn primitives and relational structures jointly, we design an end-to-end architecture with three components: (1) a convolutional neural network (CNN) backbone that extracts general visual features, (2) a concept bottleneck layer that maps these features to primitive heatmaps with differentiable spatial coordinates, and (3) a structural scoring layer that evaluates candidate spatial relations among the detected primitives. We then compute class probability from the joint evidence of its class-specific relational compositions.
  Across CUB-DG and the DomainBed benchmark suite,PARSE improves accuracy by over 4.5 percentage points on CUB-DG and remains competitive with existing DG methods on DomainBed.

</details>


### [52] [RealCam: Real-Time Novel-View Video Generation with Interactive Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06051)
*Youcan Xu,Jiaxin Shi,Zhen Wang,Wensong Song,Feifei Shao,Chen Liang,Jun Xiao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealCam是一个用于实时、交互式相机控制视频生成的新型自回归框架。它通过交叉帧上下文学习设计高保真教师模型，再利用自强制分布匹配蒸馏将其蒸馏为少步因果学生模型，并结合循环闭合数据增强（LoopAug）解决循环不一致问题，实现了比现有方法快数个数量级的推理速度，同时保持最先进的视觉质量和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式视频合成方法基于非因果的全序列处理和刚性前缀式时序拼接，导致双向注意力机制带来巨大计算延迟、二次复杂度扩展，且无法兼容实时流式或可变长度输入，亟需一种支持实时、交互式相机控制的视频生成框架。

Method: 1) 设计基于交叉帧上下文学习（Cross-frame In-context Learning）的高保真教师模型，通过交错源帧和目标帧形成同步上下文对，实现长度无关泛化和因果适配；2) 利用自强制分布匹配蒸馏（Self-Forcing with Distribution Matching Distillation）将教师模型蒸馏为少步因果学生模型，支持高效流式合成；3) 提出循环闭合数据增强（LoopAug），从现有多视图数据集中合成全局一致的循环序列以缓解闭环轨迹中的循环不一致问题。

Result: RealCam在视觉保真度和时间一致性上达到最先进水平，同时实现真正的交互式相机控制，推理速度比现有范式快数个数量级。

Conclusion: RealCam通过自回归框架、交叉帧上下文学习和蒸馏策略，成功打破了现有方法的计算瓶颈和长度限制，为实时、交互式的相机控制视频生成提供了可行的解决方案，在交互式电影制作和直播等领域具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video-to-video (V2V) generation enables dynamic viewpoint synthesis from monocular footage, holding immense potential for interactive filmmaking and live broadcasting. However, existing implicit synthesis methods fundamentally rely on non-causal, full-sequence processing and rigid prefix-style temporal concatenation. This architectural paradigm mandates bidirectional attention, resulting in prohibitive computational latency, quadratic complexity scaling, and inherent incompatibility with real-time streaming or variable-length inputs. To overcome these limitations, we introduce \texttt{RealCam}, a novel autoregressive framework for interactive, real-time camera-controlled V2V generation. We first design a high-fidelity teacher model grounded in a \textbf{Cross-frame In-context Learning} paradigm. By interleaving source and target frames into synchronized contextual pairs, our design inherently enables length-agnostic generalization and naturally facilitates causal adaptation, breaking the rigid prefix bottleneck. We then distill this teacher into a few-step causal student via Self-Forcing with Distribution Matching Distillation, enabling efficient, on-the-fly streaming synthesis. Furthermore, to mitigate severe loop inconsistency in closed-loop trajectories, we propose \textbf{Loop-Closed Data Augmentation (LoopAug)}, a novel paradigm that synthesizes globally consistent loop sequences from existing multiview datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \texttt{RealCam} achieves state-of-the-art visual fidelity and temporal consistency while enabling truly interactive camera control with orders-of-magnitude faster inference than existing paradigms. Our project page is at https://xyc-fly.github.io/RealCam/.

</details>


### [53] [PersonaGesture: Single-Reference Co-Speech Gesture Personalization for Unseen Speakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06064)
*Xiangyue Zhang,Yiyi Cai,Kunhang Li,Kaixing Yang,You Zhou,Zhengqing Li,Xuangeng Chu,Jiaxu Zhang,Haiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PersonaGesture扩散管道，通过单段参考运动实现未见说话人的共语手势个性化，无需针对每个说话人优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从单一参考中分离稳定的说话人习惯与话语特定轨迹，导致无法高效个性化未见说话人的手势生成。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：Adaptive Style Infusion（ASI）通过零初始化的残差交叉注意力注入说话人记忆token；Implicit Distribution Rectification（IDR）通过长度感知的对角仿射映射校正残差通道统计量。

Result: 在BEAT2和ZeroEGGS上，定量指标、身份控制、同音频诊断、定性对比及人类偏好评估均优于风格编码、全参考注意力及单片段微调等方法。

Conclusion: 分离去噪时说话人记忆与保守的后生成时刻校正，能有效提升未见说话人的手势个性化性能。

Abstract: We propose PersonaGesture, a diffusion-based pipeline for single-reference co-speech gesture personalization of unseen speakers. Given target speech and one motion clip from a new speaker, the model must synthesize gestures that follow the new utterance while retaining speaker-specific pose choices, without per-speaker optimization. This setting is useful for avatars and virtual agents, but it is hard because the reference mixes stable speaker habits with utterance-specific trajectories. PersonaGesture consists of two key components, Adaptive Style Infusion (ASI) and Implicit Distribution Rectification (IDR), to separate temporal identity evidence from residual statistic correction. A Style Perceiver first encodes the variable-length reference into compact speaker-memory tokens. ASI injects these tokens into denoising through zero-initialized residual cross-attention, enabling style evidence to affect motion formation without replacing the pretrained speech-to-motion prior. Building on this, IDR applies a length-aware diagonal affine map in latent space to correct residual channel-wise moments estimated from the same reference. Across BEAT2 and ZeroEGGS, we evaluate quantitative metrics, reference-identity controls, same-audio diagnostics, qualitative comparisons, and human preference. Experiments show that separating denoising-time speaker memory from conservative post-generation moment correction improves unseen-speaker personalization over collapsed style codes, full-reference attention, and one-clip finetuning. Project: https://xiangyue-zhang.github.io/PersonaGesture.

</details>


### [54] [Arena as Offline Reward: Efficient Fine-Grained Preference Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06070)
*Zhikai Li,Yue Zhao,Edward Zhongwei Zhang,Xuewen Liu,Jing Zhang,Qingyi Gu,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArenaPO方法，利用竞技场分数代替二元偏好反馈，实现无需奖励模型的细粒度偏好优化，在文本到图像扩散模型中取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的直接偏好优化（DPO）依赖二元反馈，只能对选择-拒绝对进行粗粒度建模，导致优化效果欠佳，而传统RLHF虽奖励丰富但计算成本高。

Method: 构建模型竞技场，将每个模型能力表示为高斯分布；基于截断正态分布进行潜变量推理，估计图像对间的绝对质量差距，作为细粒度反馈进行训练。

Result: 在Pick-a-Pic v2和HPD v3数据集上进行实验，ArenaPO持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ArenaPO结合了传统RLHF的丰富奖励和DPO的高效性，无需奖励模型即可实现细粒度优化，提升了T2I扩散模型的偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) effectively promotes preference alignment of text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models. To improve computational efficiency, direct preference optimization (DPO), which avoids explicit reward modeling, has been widely studied. However, its reliance on binary feedback limits it to coarse-grained modeling on chosen-rejected pairs, resulting in suboptimal optimization. In this paper, we propose ArenaPO, which leverages Arena scores as offline rewards to provide refined feedback, thus achieving efficient and fine-grained optimization without a reward model. This enables ArenaPO to benefit from both the rich rewards of traditional RLHF and the efficiency of DPO. Specifically, we first construct a model Arena in which each model's capability is represented as a Gaussian distribution, and infer these capabilities by traversing the annotated pairwise preferences. Each output image is treated as a sample from the corresponding capability distribution. Then, for a image pair, conditioned on the two capability distributions and the observed pairwise preference, the absolute quality gap is estimated using latent-variable inference based on truncated normal distribution, which serves as fine-grained feedback during training. It does not require a reward model and can be computed offline, thus introducing no additional training overhead. We conduct ArenaPO training on Pick-a-Pic v2 and HPD v3 datasets, showing that ArenaPO consistently outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [55] [MSD-Score: Multi-Scale Distributional Scoring for Reference-Free Image Caption Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06080)
*Shichao Kan,Xuyang Zhang,Haojie Zhang,Zhe Zhu,Yigang Cen,Yixiong Liang,Lianlei Shan,Linna Zhang,Zhe Qu,Jiazhi Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MSD-Score是一种无需参考的图文匹配度量方法，通过将图像块和文本词元建模为von Mises-Fisher混合分布，利用多尺度分布评分和双向KL散度来检测细粒度语义差异，在无参考指标中达到了最优的人类判断相关性，并能提供可解释的局部定位错误诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无参考图像描述评估方法主要依赖全局嵌入相似度，难以捕捉幻觉对象、属性缺失或关系错误等细粒度不匹配问题。

Method: 将图像块和文本词元嵌入建模为单位超球面上的von Mises-Fisher混合分布，通过加权双向KL散度量化语义差异，并结合全局相似度构建多尺度评分框架。

Result: 在无参考指标中达到与人类判断最优的相关性，同时提供可分解的局部定位错误诊断。

Conclusion: MSD-Score的概率建模方式不仅提升了评估准确性，还为整体相似度度量和基于裁判的评估器提供了确定性的补充信号。

Abstract: Evaluating image captions without references remains challenging because global embedding similarity often misses fine-grained mismatches such as hallucinated objects, missing attributes, or incorrect relations. We propose MSD-Score, a reference-free metric that models image patch and text token embeddings as von Mises-Fisher mixtures on the unit hypersphere. Instead of treating each modality as a single point, MSD-Score formulates image-text matching as a multi-scale distributional scoring problem. Semantic discrepancies are quantified via a weighted bi-directional KL divergence and combined with global similarity in a multi-scale framework for both single- and multi-candidate evaluations. Extensive experiments show that MSD-Score achieves state-of-the-art correlation with human judgments among reference-free metrics. Beyond accuracy, its probabilistic formulation yields transparent and decomposable diagnostics of local grounding errors, providing a deterministic complementary signal to holistic similarity metrics and judge-based evaluators.

</details>


### [56] [OpenGaFF: Open-Vocabulary Gaussian Feature Field with Codebook Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06088)
*Kunyi Li,Michael Niemeyer,Sen Wang,Stefano Gasperini,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenGaFF通过高斯特征场和结构化码本，在3D高斯溅射框架下实现了开放词汇的3D场景理解，显著提升了语义一致性和分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的开放词汇3D场景理解方法存在跨多视角观测的语义预测碎片化和空间不一致问题。

Method: 提出高斯特征场（将语义预测与几何结构显式耦合），引入结构化码本作为共享语义基元，并设计码本引导的注意力机制通过相似度匹配检索语言特征。

Result: 在标准2D和3D开放词汇基准上持续优于先前方法，分割质量更高，3D语义一致性更强。

Conclusion: OpenGaFF验证了将几何与语义强耦合、结合结构化码本能有效提升开放词汇3D场景理解的性能，且学到的码本具有语义可解释性。

Abstract: Understanding open-vocabulary 3D scenes with Gaussian-based representations remains challenging due to fragmented and spatially inconsistent semantic predictions across multi-view observations. In this paper, we present OpenGaFF, a novel framework for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding built upon 3D Gaussian Splatting. At the core of our method is a Gaussian Feature Field that models semantics as a continuous function of Gaussian geometry and appearance. By explicitly conditioning semantic predictions on geometric structure, this formulation strengthens the coupling between geometry and semantics, leading to improved spatial coherence across similar structures in 3D space. To further enforce object-level semantic consistency, we introduce a structured codebook that serves as a set of shared semantic primitives. Furthermore, a codebook-guided attention mechanism is proposed to retrieve language features via similarity matching between query embeddings and learned codebook entries, enabling robust open-vocabulary reasoning while reducing intra-object feature variance. Extensive experiments on standard 2D and 3D open-vocabulary benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches, achieving improved segmentation quality, stronger 3D semantic consistency and a semantically interpretable codebook that provides insight into the learned representation.

</details>


### [57] [Boosting Self-Supervised Tracking with Contextual Prompts and Noise Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06092)
*Yaozong Zheng,Qihua Liang,Bineng Zhong,Shuimu Zeng,Yuanliang Xue,Ning Li,Shuxiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为\tracker的自监督跟踪框架，通过双模态上下文关联机制（结合细粒度语义提示和上下文噪声），在无标签视频中学习鲁棒的跟踪表示，采用从易到难的训练策略，仅训练时使用，推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督跟踪器缺乏有效的上下文建模，基于非语义查询的上下文关联方法难以适应无标签跟踪场景，无法学习可靠的上下文线索。

Method: 提出双模态上下文关联机制：早期训练阶段，为前后向跟踪分支分配实例补丁令牌（提示）以获取跟踪知识；随训练推进，逐步注入上下文噪声扰动特征，迫使跟踪器在更复杂的特征空间中学习鲁棒表示。该机制遵循从易到难的学习原则，仅训练阶段使用。

Result: 大量实验证明了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该方法能从未标记视频中学习高质量的跟踪表示，且推理阶段保持高效。

Abstract: Learning robust contextual knowledge from unlabeled videos is essential for advancing self-supervised tracking. However, conventional self-supervised trackers lack effective context modeling, while existing context association methods based on non-semantic queries struggle to adapt to unlabeled tracking scenarios, making it difficult to learn reliable contextual cues. In this work, we propose a novel self-supervised tracking framework, named \textbf{\tracker}, which introduces a dual-modal context association mechanism that jointly leverages fine-grained semantic prompts and contextual noise to drive the model toward learning robust tracking representations. Adherent to the easy-to-hard learning principle, our contextual association mechanism operates based on two stages. During early training, instance patch tokens (prompts) are assigned to both forward and backward tracking branches to facilitate the acquisition of tracking knowledge. As training progresses, contextual noise is gradually injected into the model to perturb feature, encouraging the tracker to learn robust tracking representations in a more complex feature space. Thus, this novel contextual association mechanism enables our self-supervised model to learn high-quality tracking representations from unlabeled videos, while being applied exclusively during training to preserve efficient inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [58] [VISD: Enhancing Video Reasoning via Structured Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06094)
*Hao Lin,Kunyang Lv,Xu Jiang,Jingqi Tian,Zhongjing Du,Jiayu Ding,Qiaoman Zhang,Hongbo Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISD框架，通过结构化自蒸馏和方向-幅度解耦机制，为视频大语言模型提供细粒度令牌级监督，实现近2倍收敛加速并提升推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型训练中，序列级奖励缺乏细粒度信用分配，自蒸馏方法缺乏结构化诊断信息且与强化学习不稳定，亟需一种兼具结构化密集监督与稳定训练的框架。

Method: 提出VISD框架：1）视频感知评判模型从答案正确性、逻辑一致性、时空定位等多维度分解推理质量；2）方向-幅度解耦机制，用奖励计算的优势确定更新方向，结构化特权信号调节令牌级更新幅度；3）课程调度和EMA教师稳定策略。

Result: 在多个基准上持续优于强基线，提升答案准确性和时空定位质量，实现近2倍优化收敛加速。

Conclusion: 结构化自监督能有效提升视频大语言模型的性能与样本效率，VISD为复杂视频推理提供了一种高效、稳定的训练范式。

Abstract: Training VideoLLMs for complex reasoning remains challenging due to sparse sequence level rewards and the lack of fine grained credit assignment over long, temporally grounded reasoning trajectories. While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) provides reliable supervision, it fails to capture token level contributions, leading to inefficient learning. Conversely, existing self distillation methods offer dense supervision but lack structure and diagnostic specificity, and often interact unstably with reinforcement learning. In this work, we propose VISD, a structured self distillation framework that introduces diagnostically meaningful privileged information for video reasoning. VISD employs a video aware judge model to decompose reasoning quality into multiple dimensions, including answer correctness, logical consistency, and spatio-temporal grounding, and uses this structured feedback to guide a teacher policy for token level supervision. To stably integrate dense supervision with RL, we introduce a direction magnitude decoupling mechanism, where rollout level advantages computed from rewards determine update direction, while structured privileged signals modulate token level update magnitudes. This design enables semantically aligned and fine grained credit assignment, improving both reasoning faithfulness and training efficiency. Additionally, VISD incorporates curriculum scheduling and EMA based teacher stabilization to support robust optimization over long video sequences. Experiments on diverse benchmarks show that VISD consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving answer accuracy and spatio temporal grounding quality. Notably, VISD reaches these gains with nearly 2x faster convergence in optimization steps, highlighting the effectiveness of structured self supervision in improving both performance and sample efficiency for VideoLLMs.

</details>


### [59] [Metonymy in vision models undermines attention-based interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06095)
*Ananthu Aniraj,Cassio F. Dantas,Dino Ienco,Massimiliano Mancini,Diego Marcos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现代预训练视觉Transformer存在严重的“物体内信息泄露”问题，即每个部件编码的信息实质上包含了整个物体的信息，导致基于注意力机制的可解释部件推理方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验基于部件的视觉模型中一个基本假设——潜表征主要编码对应图像区域的信息——是否成立，揭示部件间信息泄露对模型可解释性的影响。

Method: 利用部件级属性标注，系统测量预训练视觉Transformer中物体内信息泄露程度；提出两阶段方法（先解耦特征提取，再进行部件发现）作为上限基线。

Result: 现代视觉Transformer严重违反局部性假设，部件表征存在强烈物体内信息泄露（视觉转喻）；两阶段方法可有效防止泄露，并提升属性驱动的部件发现性能。

Conclusion: 部件级表征的信息泄露是一个被忽视但严重影响可解释性的问题，两阶段方法是缓解该问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Part-based reasoning is a classical strategy to make a computer vision model directly focus on the object parts that are relevant to the downstream task. In the context of deep learning, this also serves to improve by-design interpretability, often by using part-centric attention mechanisms on top of a latent image representation provided by a standard, black-box model. This approach is based on a locality assumption: that the latent representation of an object part encodes primarily information about the corresponding image region. In this work, we test this basic assumption, measuring intra-object leakage in vision models using part-based attribute annotations. Through a comprehensive experimental evaluation, we show that modern pretrained vision transformers violate the locality assumption and exhibit a strong intra-object leakage, in which each part encodes information from the whole object, a visual metonymy that compromises the faithfulness of attention-based interpretable-by-design methods for part-based reasoning, ultimately rendering them uninterpretable. In addition, we establish an upper bound using a two-stage approach that prevents leakage by design. We then show that this inherently disentangled feature extraction improves attribute-driven part discovery on a variety of tasks, confirming the practical impact of intra-object leakage. Our results uncover a neglected issue affecting the interpretability of part-based representations, such as those in CBMs relying on part-centric concepts, highlighting that two-stage approaches offer a promising way to mitigate it.

</details>


### [60] [Pest-Thinker: Learning to Think and Reason like Entomologists via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06121)
*Xueheng Li,Yu Wang,Tao Hu,Ji Huang,Ke Cao,Qize Yang,Rui Li,Jie Zhang,Chengjun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pest-Thinker框架，通过知识驱动强化学习提升多模态大模型在农业害虫细粒度形态识别中的推理能力，构建了两个高清害虫基准数据集，结合CoT推理轨迹监督微调和GRPO优化，显著提升了域内外害虫形态理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 害虫导致的作物损失严重威胁全球粮食安全和农业可持续发展。现有MLLMs在害虫识别领域应用有限，面临种间复杂度高、种内变异性大以及专家标注数据稀缺等挑战。

Method: 1) 构建两个高清害虫基准数据集QFSD和AgriInsect，包含多样物种和专家标注的形态特征；2) 利用数据集合成CoT推理轨迹，通过SFT进行结构化学习；3) 采用GRPO算法结合新型特征奖励机制，由LLM-as-a-Judge策略评估，引导模型关注可观察的形态学证据。

Result: Pest-Thinker在域内和域外的形态理解任务上均有显著提升，向专家级别的视觉推理迈进了重要一步。

Conclusion: 知识驱动的强化学习框架能有效提升MLLMs在农业害虫细粒度识别中的表现，为智能农业害虫分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Pest-induced crop losses pose a major threat to global food security and sustainable agricultural development. While recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for visual understanding and smart agriculture, their direct application to pest recognition remains limited due to the domain's unique challenges such as high inter-species complexity, intra-species variability, and the scarcity of expert-annotated data. In this work, we introduce Pest-Thinker, a knowledge-driven reinforcement learning (RL) framework that enables MLLMs to reason over fine-grained pest morphology. We first construct two high-definition pest benchmarks, QFSD and AgriInsect, comprising diverse species and expert-annotated morphological traits. Leveraging these datasets, we synthesize Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories to facilitate structured learning of pest-specific visual cues through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). Subsequently, we employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel feature reward that guides the model to focus on observable morphological evidence, assessed by an LLM-as-a-Judge strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pest-Thinker substantially improves both in-domain and out-of-domain morphological understanding, marking a step toward expert-level visual reasoning for intelligent agricultural pest analysis. The datasets and source code are available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [61] [Continuous Expert Assembly: Instance-Conditioned Low-Rank Residuals for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06127)
*Haisen He,Xiangyu Zou,SongLin Dong,Heng Li,Yihong Gong,Zhiheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为连续专家组装（CEA）的逐token动态参数化框架，用于全能图像恢复。该方法通过轻量级交叉注意力超适配器生成低秩路由基和残差方向，每个空间token通过密集符号点积亲和度自主组装残差更新，无需外部提示、静态专家库或离散Top-k选择，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能图像恢复方法存在两个核心问题：全局条件调制会瓶颈局部退化证据，而静态专家路由要么产生同质更新要么依赖不稳定的稀疏分配。需要一种既能适应空间非均匀退化又不依赖离散选择的动态参数化方法。

Method: 提出CEA框架，核心是Cross-Attention Hyper-Adapter，它通过探测中间空间特征来合成实例条件化的低秩路由基和残差方向。每个空间token通过计算与所有秩分量的密集符号点积亲和度，自主组装自己的残差更新。整个过程无需外部提示、静态专家库和离散Top-k选择，且从线性注意力视角提供了可解释性。

Result: 在AIO-3、AIO-5和CDD-11三个基准上，CEA在平均恢复质量上超越了基于提示、描述符和专家的强基线方法，在空间变化和组合退化场景中增益最为显著，同时保持了参数、FLOP和运行时效率的优势。

Conclusion: CEA通过逐token动态参数化的方式有效解决了全局条件瓶颈和静态专家路由的局限，其密集注意力式的组装规则兼具高效性和可解释性，为全能图像恢复提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Real-world image degradation is often unknown, spatially non-uniform, and compositional, requiring all-in-one restoration models to adapt a single set of weights to diverse local corruption patterns without test-time degradation labels. Existing methods typically modulate a shared backbone with global prompts or degradation descriptors, or route features through predefined expert pools. However, compact global conditioning can bottleneck localized degradation evidence, while static expert routing may produce homogeneous updates or rely on unstable sparse assignments. We propose \textbf{Continuous Expert Assembly} (CEA), a token-wise dynamic parameterization framework for all-in-one image restoration. CEA employs a lightweight \textbf{Cross-Attention Hyper-Adapter} to probe intermediate spatial features and synthesize instance-conditioned low-rank routing bases and residual directions. Each spatial token then assembles its own residual update via dense signed dot-product affinities over the generated rank-wise components, avoiding external prompts, static expert banks, and discrete Top-
  selection. The resulting assembly rule also admits a linear-attention perspective, making its dense token-wise routing behavior transparent. Experiments on AIO-3, AIO-5, and CDD-11 show that CEA improves average restoration quality over strong prompt-, descriptor-, and expert-based baselines, with the clearest gains on spatially varying and compositional degradations, while maintaining favorable parameter, FLOP, and runtime efficiency.

</details>


### [62] [Autoregressive Visual Generation Needs a Prologue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06137)
*Bowen Zheng,Weijian Luo,Guang Yang,Colin Zhang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Prologue通过在视觉token序列前添加一组专门用于生成任务的前言token，实现自回归图像生成中重建与生成任务的解耦，在不影响重建质量的前提下显著提升生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像生成中，视觉token需要同时兼顾重建（准确还原图像）和生成（产生逼真图像）两个目标，这两者之间存在本质冲突，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 提出Prologue方法：生成一组小规模的前言token（prologue tokens），将其前置在视觉token序列之前。前言token仅用自回归交叉熵损失训练，而视觉token专注于重建任务，实现生成与重建的显式解耦。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上，Prologue-Base将gFID从21.01降至10.75（无分类器引导），重建几乎不变；Prologue-Large达到rFID 0.99、gFID 1.46。前言token展现出语义结构，16个token线性探测Top-1达35.88%，远超标准tokenizer的23.71%。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立的可学习生成表示，可以在保持原始表示不变的情况下提升生成质量，为自回归图像生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Prologue, an approach to bridging the reconstruction-generation gap in autoregressive (AR) image generation. Instead of modifying visual tokens to satisfy both reconstruction and generation, Prologue generates a small set of prologue tokens prepended to the visual token sequence. These prologue tokens are trained exclusively with the AR cross-entropy (CE) loss, while visual tokens remain dedicated to reconstruction. This decoupled design lets us optimize generation through the AR model's true distribution without affecting reconstruction quality, which we further formalize from an ELBO perspective. On ImageNet 256x256, Prologue-Base reduces gFID from 21.01 to 10.75 without classifier-free guidance while keeping reconstruction almost unchanged; Prologue-Large reaches a competitive rFID of 0.99 and gFID of 1.46 using a standard AR model without auxiliary semantic supervision. Interestingly, driven only by AR gradients, prologue tokens exhibit emergent semantic structure: linear probing on 16 prologue tokens reaches 35.88% Top-1, far above the 23.71% of the first 16 tokens from a standard tokenizer; resampling with fixed prologue tokens preserves a similar high-level semantic layout. Our results suggest a new direction: generation quality can be improved by introducing a separate learned generative representation while leaving the original representation intact.

</details>


### [63] [AI-Generated Images: What Humans and Machines See When They Look at the Same Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06143)
*Silvia Poletti,Justin Ilyes,Marcel Hasenbalg,David Fischinger,Martin Boyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何让AI生成图像检测器提供人类可理解的解释，通过构建多种检测器架构、整合16种XAI方法，并结合100名参与者的调查数据，提出了一种优先考虑人类理解的可解释性评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI被滥用于在线虚假信息活动，迫切需要透明且可解释的检测系统。

Method: 开发了多种架构和微调策略的检测器，在AIText2Image数据集上训练；整合16种XAI方法；通过100名参与者的调查，使用文本和视觉响应来优化和评估视觉解释。

Result: 为AI生成图像检测提供了视觉-语言线索的见解，并衡量了XAI输出与人类偏好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架从人类视角提升了XAI方法的清晰度，有助于构建更可信的虚假内容检测系统。

Abstract: The misuse of generative AI in online disinformation campaigns highlights the urgent need for transparent and explainable detection systems. In this work, we investigate how detectors for AI-generated images can be more effective in providing human-understandable explanations for their predictions. To this end, we develop a suite of detectors with various architectures and fine-tuning strategies, trained on our large-scale photorealistic fake image dataset, AIText2Image, and assess their performance on state-of-the-art text-to-image AI generators. We integrate 16 different explainable AI (XAI) methods into our detection framework, and the visual explanations are comprehensively refined and evaluated through a novel approach that prioritizes human understanding of AI-generated images, using both textual and visual responses collected from a survey of 100 participants. This framework offers insights into visual-language cues in fake image detection and into the clarity of XAI methods from a human perspective, measuring the alignment of XAI outputs with human preferences.

</details>


### [64] [Learning Discrete Autoregressive Priors with Wasserstein Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06148)
*Bowen Zheng,Yihong Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: wAR-Tok通过在分词器训练中加入分布级先验匹配信号（基于Wasserstein梯度流更新），解决了传统两阶段图像分词器中分词器与自回归先验模型不匹配的问题，在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升了生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段训练的分词器与后续自回归先验模型相互独立，导致学习到的token虽然重建效果好，但对自回归模型从左到右的预测不够友好，存在先验不一致问题。

Method: 基于Tripartite Variational Consistency (TVC)框架分析，在分词器训练中加入分布级先验匹配信号；使用Wasserstein梯度流更新优化该信号，对硬类别token简化为辅助AR模型与目标AR先验之间的token级对比，无需反向传播。

Result: wAR-Tok在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上降低了自回归损失，提升了生成FID指标，同时保持可比较的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过在分词器训练阶段引入先验一致性约束，可以有效弥合分词器与自回归先验之间的鸿沟，提升图像生成性能。

Abstract: Discrete image tokenizers are commonly trained in two stages: first for reconstruction, and then with a prior model fitted to the frozen token sequences. This decoupling leaves the tokenizer unaware of the model that will later generate its tokens. As a result, the learned tokens may preserve image information well but still be difficult for an autoregressive (AR) prior to predict from left to right. We analyze this mismatch using Tripartite Variational Consistency (TVC), which decomposes latent-variable learning into three consistency conditions: conditional-likelihood consistency, prior consistency, and posterior consistency. TVC shows that two-stage training preserves the reconstruction side but leaves prior consistency outside the tokenizer objective: the overall token distribution is fixed before the AR prior participates in training. Motivated by this view, we add a distribution-level prior-matching signal during tokenizer training, while keeping the reconstruction objective unchanged. We optimize this signal with a Wasserstein-gradient-flow update. For hard categorical tokens, the update reduces to a token-level contrast between an auxiliary AR model that tracks the tokenizer's current token distribution and the target AR prior. It requires only forward passes through the two AR models and does not backpropagate through either of them. The resulting tokenizer, wAR-Tok, reduces AR loss and improves generation FID on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet at comparable reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Forgetting in Continual Medical Image Segmentation: A Comprehensive Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06160)
*Bomin Wang,Hangqi Zhou,Yibo Gao,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向临床场景的持续医学图像分割基准研究，定义了三种临床驱动的场景（Domain-CL、Class-CL、Organ-CL），并引入包括可塑性、前向泛化能力等多维评估框架，实验表明重放方法在稳定性与可塑性之间平衡最佳，而前向泛化能力仍是研究中被忽视的重要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前持续医学图像分割研究存在三个主要问题：场景定义不够标准化以匹配真实临床需求、现有研究过度关注遗忘问题而忽视可塑性等关键属性、缺乏对现有方法的全面基准评估。

Method: 定义了三种临床驱动场景：Domain-CL（跨中心域偏移）、Class-CL（递增解剖结构分割）、Organ-CL（跨器官分割）；并构建了包含通用性能、遗忘、可塑性、前向泛化能力、参数效率、重放负担的多维评估框架。

Result: 实验表明：重放方法在稳定性和可塑性间取得最佳平衡；参数隔离方法能有效减少遗忘但模型体积增大；前向泛化能力是当前研究严重不足的方面。

Conclusion: 开发能同时满足所有需求的模型仍具挑战性，讨论了相关学习范式并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for deploying medical image segmentation models in clinical environments where imaging domains, anatomical targets, and diagnostic tasks evolve over time. However, continual segmentation still faces three main challenges. First, the scenarios for this task remain insufficiently standardized for real-world clinical settings. Second, existing research has been primarily focused on mitigating forgetting, overlooking the other essential properties such as plasticity. Third, a benchmark work with comprehensive evaluation on existing methods is stll desirable. To address these gaps, we present such benchmark study of continual medical image segmentation. We first define three clinically motivated scenarios, namely Domain-CL, Class-CL, and Organ-CL, to respectively capture the cross-center domain shift, the incremental anatomical structure segmentation, and the cross-organ segmentation. We then introduce an evaluation framework that measures not only general performance and forgetting, but also plasticity, forward generalizability, parameter efficiency, and replay burden. The results, from extensive experiments with representative CL methods, showed that it was still challenging to develop a model that could satisfy all the requirements simultaneously. Nevertheless, these studies also suggested that the replay-based methods achieve the best overall balance between stability and plasticity, the parameter-isolation methods should be effective at reducing forgetting, though at the cost of increased model size, and the forward generalizability remain a significantly understudied aspect of this research field. Finally, we discuss related learning paradigms and outline future directions for continual medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [66] [DynT2I-Eval: A Dynamic Evaluation Framework for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06170)
*Juntong Wang,Jiarui Wang,Huiyu Duan,Lewei Li,Guangtao Zhai,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynT2I-Eval，一种全自动动态评估框架，通过结构化视觉语义空间持续生成新提示，避免T2I模型评估中的过拟合和基准污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I基准依赖固定提示集，容易因公开复用导致过拟合和基准污染，亟需动态评估方法。

Method: 构建结构化视觉语义空间，将提示分解为可控维度（主体、逻辑约束、环境、组合），通过任务特定空间和难度感知采样持续生成新提示；采用提示条件化成对比较、动态调度器、微批聚合和加权贝叶斯更新维护在线排行榜。

Result: 实验表明持续刷新提示减少了提示集特定调优的影响，排名框架在冷启动收敛、新模型发现和长期排名保真度之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: DynT2I-Eval提供了一种鲁棒的动态评估协议，有效缓解了传统固定基准的固有问题。

Abstract: Existing text-to-image (T2I) benchmarks largely rely on fixed prompt sets, leaving them vulnerable to overfitting and benchmark contamination once publicly released and repeatedly reused. In this work, we propose DynT2I-Eval, a fully automated dynamic evaluation framework for T2I models. It constructs a structured visual semantic space from long-form descriptions, decomposing prompts into controllable dimensions (e.g., subject, logical constraint, environment, and composition). This enables the continuous generation of fresh prompts via task-specific spaces and difficulty-aware sampling. DynT2I-Eval evaluates model performance across text alignment, perceptual quality, and aesthetics. Heterogeneous outputs are unified into prompt-conditioned pairwise comparisons, allowing a dynamic scheduler, micro-batch aggregation, and weighted Bayesian updates to maintain a stable online leaderboard despite changing prompt distributions and model injection. Experiments with independently sampled prompt streams demonstrate that continually refreshed prompts provide a robust evaluation protocol, reducing the impact of prompt-set-specific tuning. Simulations and ablations further confirm that the proposed ranking framework achieves a strong balance among cold-start convergence, late-entry discovery, and long-run ranking fidelity.

</details>


### [67] [Retina-RAG: Retrieval-Augmented Vision-Language Modeling for Joint Retinal Diagnosis and Clinical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06173)
*Abdelrahman Zaian,Sheethal Bhat,Mohamed Abdalkader,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Retina-RAG 是一个低成本模块化框架，联合执行 DR 分级、ME 检测和报告生成，在单张消费级 GPU 上运行，性能优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DR 自动筛查系统大多仅做图像级分类，缺乏临床结构化报告生成能力，且现有模型易产生幻觉。

Method: 采用解耦架构：高性能视网膜分类器 + 通过 LoRA 适配的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 视觉语言模型，并引入 RAG 模块注入眼科知识以提高诊断一致性。

Result: DR 分级 F1=0.731，ME 检测 F1=0.948，显著优于 Qwen zero-shot（0.096, 0.732）和 MMed-RAG（0.541, 0.641）；报告生成 ROUGE-L=0.429，SBERT 相似度=0.884。

Conclusion: 证明了在有限计算资源下也能实现具有临床结构化输出的视网膜 AI 系统。

Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness among working-age adults worldwide, yet most automated screening systems are limited to image-level classification and lack clinically structured reporting. We propose Retina-RAG, a low-cost modular framework that jointly performs DR severity grading, macular edema (ME) detection, and report generation. The architecture decouples a high-performance retinal classifier and a parameter-efficient vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct) adapted via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), enabling flexible component integration. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) module injects curated ophthalmic knowledge together with structured classifier outputs at inference time to improve diagnostic consistency and reduce hallucinations. Retina-RAG achieves an F1-score of 0.731 for DR grading and 0.948 for ME detection, substantially outperforming zero-shot Qwen (0.096, 0.732) and MMed-RAG (0.541, 0.641) on a retinal disease detection dataset with captions. For report generation, Retina-RAG attains ROUGE-L 0.429 and SBERT similarity 0.884, exceeding all baselines. The full framework operates on a single consumer-grade GPU, demonstrating that clinically structured retinal AI can be achieved with modest computational resources.

</details>


### [68] [SuperFace: Preference-Aligned Facial Expression Estimation Beyond Pseudo Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06179)
*Zejian Kang,Xuanyang Xu,Wentao Yang,Kai Zheng,Yuanchen Fei,Hongyuan Zou,Hui Shan,Shuo Yang,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperFace框架，通过人类偏好反馈优化ARKit面部表情系数预测，替代传统依赖伪标签的监督学习方法，实现更逼真的面部动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARKit面部表情系数预测方法依赖Live Link Face等软件提供的伪标签进行监督学习，但这些伪标签存在噪声激活、系数偏差、动作缺失或不准确等问题，导致模型模仿了不完美的伪标签而非优化感知层面的表情 fidelity。

Method: 提出SuperFace偏好驱动框架：将软件估计的系数作为初始化，通过人类对渲染面部表情的偏好反馈进一步优化系数预测，将模型从伪标签模仿转向与人类感知对齐的优化。

Result: 实验表明，SuperFace在表情 fidelity 上优于使用Live Link Face监督的基线方法，验证了偏好驱动优化在语义面部动作预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 偏好驱动框架能有效提升ARKit面部表情估计的视觉真实感和表现力，替代传统伪标签监督学习范式，为面部动画生成提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Accurate facial estimation is crucial for realistic digital human animation, and ARKit blendshape coefficients offer an interpretable representation by mapping facial motions to semantic animation controls. However, learning high-quality ARKit coefficient prediction remains limited by the absence of reliable ground-truth supervision. Existing methods typically rely on capture software such as Live Link Face to provide pseudo labels, which may contain noisy activations, biased coefficient magnitudes, and missing or inaccurate facial actions. Consequently, models trained with supervised learning tend to reproduce imperfect pseudo labels rather than optimize for perceptual expression fidelity. In this paper, we propose SuperFace, a preference-driven framework that moves ARKit facial expression estimation from pseudo-label imitation toward human-aligned perceptual optimization. Instead of treating software-estimated coefficients as fixed ground truth, SuperFace uses them only as an initialization and further improves coefficient prediction through human preference feedback on rendered facial expressions. By aligning the model with perceptual judgments rather than numerical pseudo labels, SuperFace enables more visually faithful and expressive facial animation. Experiments show that SuperFace improves expression fidelity over Live Link Face supervision, demonstrating the effectiveness of preference-driven optimization for semantic facial action prediction.

</details>


### [69] [EA-WM: Event-Aware Generative World Model with Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06192)
*Zhaoyang Yang,Yurun Jin,Lizhe Qi,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EA-WM提出了一种事件感知生成式世界模型，通过将运动学控制信号投影为结构化视觉动作场，并引入事件感知双向融合模块，显著提升了视频生成中机器人空间几何与交互动力学的保真度，在WorldArena基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型主要将视频生成视为策略学习的辅助表征，未能充分探索利用动作信号引导视频合成这一逆问题，导致生成的rollout中机器人空间几何和精细交互动力学保真度不足。

Method: 将关节/末端执行器动作与运动学状态投影到目标相机视角，形成结构化运动学-视觉动作场；引入事件感知双向融合模块，调节跨分支注意力以捕捉物体状态变化与交互动态。

Result: 在WorldArena基准测试上实现了最先进的性能，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: EA-WM有效闭环了运动学控制与视觉感知之间的连接，证明了结构化动作场表示与事件感知融合对提升生成式世界模型质量的关键作用。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models provide powerful spatiotemporal generative priors, making them a natural foundation for robotic world models. While recent world-action models jointly optimize future videos and actions, they predominantly treat video generation as an auxiliary representation for policy learning. Consequently, they insufficiently explore the inverse problem: leveraging action signals to guide video synthesis, thereby often failing to preserve precise robot spatial geometry and fine-grained robot-object interaction dynamics in the generated rollouts. To bridge this gap, we present EA-WM, an Event-Aware Generative World Model that effectively closes the loop between kinematic control and visual perception. Rather than injecting joint or end-effector actions as abstract, low-dimensional tokens, EA-WM projects actions and kinematic states directly into the target camera view as Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields. To fully exploit this geometrically grounded representation, we introduce event-aware bidirectional fusion blocks that modulate cross-branch attention, capturing object state changes and interaction dynamics. Evaluated on the comprehensive WorldArena benchmark, EA-WM achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing baselines by a significant margin.

</details>


### [70] [Taming the Entropy Cliff: Variable Codebook Size Quantization for Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06207)
*Bowen Zheng,Weijian Luo,Guang Yang,Colin Zhang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现离散视觉分词器中固定码本大小设计存在“熵悬崖”问题：序列中前几个位置后，条件熵急剧下降导致后续位置变成记忆问题。为此提出变长码本量化（VCQ），让码本大小沿序列单调增长，在ImageNet上显著提升性能（gFID从27.98降至14.80），且小码本位置自然形成语义层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散视觉分词器默认所有位置共享相同大小的码本，研究者试图通过扩大码本大小K来提升重建性能，但这种恒定码本设计存在信息论上的根本局限。作者发现训练集的逐位置条件熵沿序列衰减极快，导致大部分位置实际上变成了记忆问题，而非真正的建模。

Method: 提出变长码本量化（VCQ），码本大小K_t沿序列从K_min=2单调增长至K_max，不改变损失函数、参数量和自回归训练流程。这利用了早期位置条件熵低的特点，在保持总容量的同时更有效地分配码本资源。

Result: 基础版VCQ在ImageNet 256×256上将gFID从27.98降至14.80；扩展版以684M参数量达到gFID 1.71，无需语义正则化或因果对齐等额外技术；仅用前10个token的线性探测达到43.8%的ImageNet top-1准确率（均匀码本为27.1%）。

Conclusion: 重要的不仅是码本的总容量，还有容量如何分布和组织。VCQ通过熵感知的码本分配，自然实现了粗到细的语义层次结构，为视觉自回归建模提供了更高效的编码范式。

Abstract: Most discrete visual tokenizers rely on a default design: every position in the sequence shares the same codebook. Researchers try to scale the codebook size $K$ to get better reconstruction performance. Such a constant-codebook design hits a fundamental information-theoretic limit. We observe that the per-position conditional entropy of the training set decays so quickly along the sequence that, after a few positions, the conditional distribution becomes essentially deterministic. On ImageNet with $K=16384$, this happens within only 2 out of 256 positions, turning the remaining 254 into a memorization problem. We call this phenomenon the Entropy Cliff and formalize it with a simple expression: $t^{*} = \lceil \log_2 N / \log_2 K \rceil$. Interestingly, this phenomenon is not observed in language, as its natural structure keeps the effective entropy per position well below the codebook capacity. To address this, we propose Variable Codebook Size Quantization (VCQ), where the codebook size $K_t$ grows monotonically along the sequence from $K_{\min}=2$ to $K_{\max}$, leaving the loss function, parameter count, and AR training procedure unchanged. With a vanilla autoregressive Transformer and standard next-token prediction, a base version of VCQ reduces gFID w/o CFG from 27.98 to 14.80 on ImageNet $256\times256$ over the baseline. Scaled up, it reaches gFID 1.71 with 684M autoregressive parameters, without any extra training techniques such as semantic regularization or causal alignment. The extreme information bottleneck at $K_{\min}=2$ naturally induces a coarse-to-fine semantic hierarchy: a linear probe on only the first 10 tokens reaches 43.8% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet, compared to 27.1% for uniform codebooks. Ultimately, these results show that what matters is not only the total capacity of the codebook, but also how that capacity is distributed and organized.

</details>


### [71] [Differentiable Adaptive 4D Structured Illumination for Joint Capture of Shape and Reflectance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06214)
*Huakeng Ding,Yaowen Chen,Kun Zhou,Hongzhi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微分框架，通过自适应计算4D光照条件，结合统一的空间-角度结构光和单相机，实现物体形状与反射率的高效同步采集。方法基于直方图的像素级概率模型，通过深度不确定性驱动的损失函数自适应选择下一光照条件，最后通过微调优化深度图和反射率参数图。在多种物理物体上验证了有效性，深度结果优于现有技术，反射率结果与真实照片相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在同时采集物体形状和反射率时，通常需要大量光照条件或复杂的硬件设置，效率和质量难以兼顾。本文旨在通过自适应光照策略，以最少的测量次数实现高质量的深度和反射率同步重建。

Method: 1) 建立基于直方图的像素级概率模型，联合建模深度和反射率的不确定性；2) 通过可微分损失函数将下一光照条件与深度不确定性降低联系起来，实现自适应光照选择；3) 每次新结构光照投射后，用图像测量更新像素级不确定性；4) 最后采用微调方法，通过最小化物理测量与模拟结果之间的差异，联合优化深度图和反射率参数图。

Result: 在形状和外观差异较大的物理物体上验证了有效性。深度重建结果优于最新技术，反射率重建结果与真实照片对比达到可比较的水平。

Conclusion: 提出的可微分自适应光照框架能够高效同步获取物体形状和反射率，深度重建质量领先现有方法，反射率重建具有实用性，为主动视觉中的自适应采集提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a differentiable framework to adaptively compute 4D illumination conditions with respect to an object, for efficient, high-quality simultaneous acquisition of its shape and reflectance, with a unified spatial-angular structured light and a single camera. Using a simple histogram-based pixel-level probability model for depth and reflectance, we differentiably link the next illumination condition(s) with a loss that encourages the reduction in depth uncertainty. As new structured illumination is cast, corresponding image measurements are used to update the uncertainty at each pixel. Finally, a fine-tuning-based approach reconstructs the depth map and reflectance parameter maps, by minimizing the differences between all physical measurements and their simulated counterparts. The effectiveness of our framework is demonstrated on physical objects with wide variations in shape and appearance. Our depth results compare favorably with state-of-the-art techniques, while our reflectance results are comparable when validated against photographs.

</details>


### [72] [Look Beyond Saliency: Low-Attention Guided Dual Encoding for Video Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06229)
*Faisal Aljehrai,Mohammed A. Alkhrashi,Alreem Almuhrij,Sarah Abuhimed,Noorh Aldossary,Abdullah Aldwyish,Raied Aljadaany,Huda Alamri,Muhammad Kamran J Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种逆注意力嵌入机制，通过捕捉被视觉编码器忽略的背景区域来提升密集场景下的视频语义检索性能，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉编码器在密集拥挤场景中倾向于关注显著前景区域，而忽略了上下文重要的背景区域，导致语义检索性能不佳。

Method: 提出逆注意力嵌入（Inverse Attention Embedding）机制，显式捕捉被忽略的背景区域，并将其与传统视觉嵌入相结合，无需额外训练即可提升性能。

Result: 在视频语义检索任务中，召回率取得了优于现有方法的提升；消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 逆注意力嵌入机制能够有效弥补传统视觉编码器对背景区域关注不足的问题，是一种轻量且有效的语义检索增强方案。

Abstract: Video semantic search in densely crowded scenes remains a challenging task due to visual encoders tendency to prioritize salient foreground regions while neglecting contextually important, background areas. We propose an Inverse Attention Embedding mechanism that explicitly captures and highlights these overlooked regions. By combining inverse attention embeddings with traditional visual embeddings, our method significantly enhances semantic retrieval performance without additional training. Initial experiments and ablation studies demonstrate promising improvements over existing approaches in recall for video semantic search in crowded environments.

</details>


### [73] [Spark3R: Asymmetric Token Reduction Makes Fast Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06270)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Qiankun Gao,Haijie Li,Yanmin Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spark3R是一个无需训练的加速框架，通过解耦查询令牌和键值令牌的压缩策略（查询令牌使用组内合并，键值令牌使用轻量剪枝），为前馈3D重建模型实现最高28倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型在扩展到数百或数千帧视频输入时，全局注意力层的二次复杂度导致计算成本过高；现有令牌合并方法对查询令牌和键值令牌采用统一压缩策略，忽略了它们在3D重建中功能角色的差异。

Method: 提出Spark3R框架，解耦查询令牌与键值令牌的压缩：查询令牌采用组内令牌合并（intra-group token merging），键值令牌采用轻量令牌剪枝（lightweight token pruning），并自适应调整不同层的键值缩减因子。

Result: 在VGGT、π³、Depth-Anything-3等多个预训练模型上，处理1000帧视频输入时实现最高28倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: Spark3R作为即插即用的训练无关加速框架，有效提升了前馈3D重建模型在长视频输入上的效率-质量平衡，为大规模3D视频重建提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models based on Vision Transformers can directly estimate scene geometry and camera poses from a small set of input images, but scaling them to video inputs with hundreds or thousands of frames remains challenging due to the quadratic cost of global attention layers. Recent token-merging methods accelerate these models by compressing the token sequence within the global attention layers, but they apply a uniform reduction to query tokens and key-value tokens, ignoring their functionally distinct roles in 3D reconstruction. In this work, we identify a key property of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models: query tokens encode view-specific geometric requests and are sensitive to compression, while key-value tokens represent shared scene context and tolerate aggressive compression. Guided by this insight, we propose Spark3R, a training-free acceleration framework that decouples the compression of query tokens and key-value tokens by assigning distinct reduction factors, with intra-group token merging applied to query tokens and lightweight token pruning to key-value tokens. Additionally, Spark3R adaptively adjusts the key-value reduction factor across layers, further improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. As a plug-and-play framework requiring no retraining, Spark3R integrates directly into multiple pretrained feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, including VGGT, $π^3$, and Depth-Anything-3, and achieves up to $28\times$ speedup on 1,000-frame inputs while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [74] [On-Orbit Real-Time Wildfire Detection Under On-Board Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06273)
*Matthias Rötzer,Veronika Pörtge,Martin Ickerott,Jayendra Praveen Kumar Chorapalli,Dimitri Scheftelowitsch,Max Bereczky,Dmitry Rashkovetsky,Sai Manoj Appalla,Julia Gottfriedsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个部署在九颗商业热红外卫星星座上的在轨野火检测系统，在严苛的联合约束下（模型<1MB、Jetson Xavier NX上TensorRT FP16推理<150ms、端到端预警<10分钟）运行。系统使用未校准的MWIR单波段图像（200m分辨率），通过DenseMAE自监督预训练方法，在极端类别不平衡下实现0.699 AP和0.744 Fire-F1，优于监督基线（0.650 AP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MODIS、VIIRS等主流野火监测系统依赖于上下文热阈值处理管线，无法有效处理亚像素或单像素热异常以及极端类别不平衡问题。同时，在轨部署面临模型体积小、推理速度快、端到端延迟低等多重严苛约束，需要设计轻量级且高精度的检测方案。

Method: 使用专有的九卫星MWIR数据集，对比了DenseMAE（密集掩码自编码器）和DenseMAE+EMA（指数移动平均蒸馏）两种自监督表示学习方法。通过线性探测和全分布像素级平均精度（AP）评估表示质量，并在极端类别不平衡下训练紧凑的下游检测模型。

Result: DenseMAE预训练的最快模型在65.34ms延迟、0.52MB引擎下达到0.640 AP和0.69 Fire-F1；最佳配置在<1MB下达到0.699 AP和0.744 Fire-F1，优于监督基线的0.650 AP。

Conclusion: DenseMAE自监督预训练能够使紧凑下游模型在延迟-精度帕累托前沿上取得优异表现，在无需剪枝或压缩的情况下，满足在轨部署的严苛约束并超越传统监督方法。
